00018: def add(parent, name, node):
00019:     parent.include(name, node)
00020:     return node
00021: 
00022: def ring(parent, name, center, radius, tube, plane="xz",
00023:          color=chrome, steps=24):
00024:     pts = []
00025:     for i in range(steps):
00026:         a = 2 * math.pi * i / steps
00027:         c, s = radius * math.cos(a), radius * math.sin(a)
00028:         if plane == "yz":
00029:             p = (center[0], center[1]+c, center[2]+s)
00030:         elif plane == "xy":
00031:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1]+s, center[2])
00032:         else:
00033:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1], center[2]+s)
00034:         pts.append(p)
00035:     result = Asset(name)
00036:     for i in range(steps):
00037:         add(result, "segment_%d" % i,
00038:             rod(radius=tube, start=pts[i],
00039:                 end=pts[(i+1) % steps], color=color))
00040:     add(parent, name, result)
00041:     return result

00278: def noise_material(name, colors, scale, roughness=.7, bump_strength=0):
00279:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00280:     mat.use_nodes = True
00281:     n, links = mat.node_tree.nodes, mat.node_tree.links
00282:     bs = n.get("Principled BSDF")
00283:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00284:     tex = n.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00285:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = scale
00286:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00287:     ramp = n.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00288:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .35
00289:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*colors[0], 1)
00290:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .65
00291:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*colors[1], 1)
00292:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], ramp.inputs["Fac"])
00293:     links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00294:     if bump_strength:
00295:         bump = n.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00296:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = bump_strength
00297:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .001
00298:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00299:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00300:     return mat
00301:

00312: def world_bounds(obj):
00313:     points = [obj.matrix_world @ Vector(p) for p in obj.bound_box]
00314:     low = Vector(tuple(min(p[i] for p in points) for i in range(3)))
00315:     high = Vector(tuple(max(p[i] for p in points) for i in range(3)))
00316:     return (low+high)*.5, high-low
00317: 
00318: for obj in list(bpy.context.scene.objects):
00319:     if obj.type != "MESH":
00320:         continue
00321:     center, dims = world_bounds(obj)
00322:     counter = (abs(dims.x-2.65)<.015 and
00323:                abs(dims.y-1.30)<.015 and abs(center.z-.89)<.015)
00324:     splash = (abs(dims.x-2.65)<.015 and
00325:               abs(dims.y-.025)<.01 and abs(center.z-.995)<.015)
00326:     if counter or splash:
00327:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00328:         obj.data.materials.append(granite)
00329:     elif (abs(center.x-rollx)<.12 and abs(center.z-rollz)<.08
00330:           and dims.z>.45):
00331:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00332:         obj.data.materials.append(paper_mat)
00333: 
00334:     # Replace the cloth proxy within its existing assembly by a folded surface.
00335:     if (abs(center.x-.10)<.005 and abs(center.y+.145)<.005
00336:         and abs(dims.x-.24)<.005 and abs(dims.z-.55)<.005):
00337:         verts, faces = [], []
00338:         cols, rows = 24, 18
00339:         for j in range(rows+1):
00340:             v = j/rows
00341:             for i in range(cols+1):
00342:                 u = i/cols
00343:                 width = .145 + .095*math.sin(math.pi*v*.78)
00344:                 x = .10 + (u-.5)*width - .035*v
00345:                 hem = .045*math.cos(u*math.pi*2) + .02*u
00346:                 z = 1.72 - v*(.57+hem)
00347:                 y = -.145 + .022*math.sin(u*math.pi*7+.5*v)
00348:                 y -= .018*math.sin(math.pi*v)
00349:                 verts.append((x, y, z))
00350:         for j in range(rows):
00351:             for i in range(cols):
00352:                 a = j*(cols+1)+i
00353:                 faces.append((a, a+1, a+cols+2, a+cols+1))
00354:         mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new("Continuous draped towel")
00355:         mesh.from_pydata(
00356:             [tuple(obj.matrix_world.inverted() @ Vector(p)) for p in verts],
00357:             [], faces)
00358:         mesh.update()
00359:         obj.data = mesh
00360:         mesh.materials.append(cloth_mat)
00361:         for polygon in mesh.polygons:
00362:             polygon.use_smooth = True
00363:         solid = obj.modifiers.new("Cloth thickness", "SOLIDIFY")
00364:         solid.thickness = .004
00365:         sub = obj.modifiers.new("Soft cloth folds", "SUBSURF")
00366:         sub.levels = 1
00367:         sub.render_levels = 1
00368: 
00369:     for mat in obj.data.materials:
00370:         if mat and mat.use_nodes and mat not in (granite, paper_mat, cloth_mat):
00371:             bs = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")

00028: def ring(parent, name, center, radius, tube, plane="xz", color=chrome, steps=24):
00029:     pts = []
00030:     for i in range(steps):
00031:         a = 2 * math.pi * i / steps
00032:         if plane == "yz":
00033:             p = (center[0], center[1]+radius*math.cos(a),
00034:                  center[2]+radius*math.sin(a))
00035:         elif plane == "xy":
00036:             p = (center[0]+radius*math.cos(a),
00037:                  center[1]+radius*math.sin(a), center[2])
00038:         else:
00039:             p = (center[0]+radius*math.cos(a), center[1],
00040:                  center[2]+radius*math.sin(a))
00041:         pts.append(p)
00042:     for i in range(steps):
00043:         add(parent, name+str(i), rod(radius=tube, start=pts[i],
00044:             end=pts[(i+1) % steps], color=color))

00245: # Procedural materials: fine granite flecks and paper/cloth surface variation.
00246: def noise_material(name, colors, scale, roughness=.7, bump_strength=0):
00247:     mat=bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00248:     mat.use_nodes=True
00249:     nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00250:     links=mat.node_tree.links
00251:     bs=nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00252:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=roughness
00253:     tex=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00254:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value=scale
00255:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value=2.0
00256:     ramp=nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00257:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.25
00258:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=(*colors[0],1)
00259:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.77
00260:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=(*colors[1],1)
00261:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs["Fac"])
00262:     links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00263:     if bump_strength:
00264:         bump=nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00265:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value=bump_strength
00266:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value=.002
00267:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00268:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00269:     return mat
00270: 
00271: granite=noise_material("Speckled warm granite",
00272:     ((.075,.070,.055),(.56,.51,.40)),210,.64,.22)
00273: paper_mat=noise_material("Subtle paper texture",
00274:     ((.83,.80,.70),(.96,.93,.84)),145,.92,.08)

00276: # Identify material targets by their positions and dimensions rather than mesh names.
00277: for obj in list(bpy.context.scene.objects):
00278:     if obj.type != 'MESH':
00279:         continue
00280:     center=obj.matrix_world.translation
00281:     dims=obj.dimensions
00282:     if dims.x>2.0 and center.z<1.2:
00283:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00284:         obj.data.materials.append(granite)
00285:     elif abs(center.x-rollx)<.12 and abs(center.z-rollz)<.08:
00286:         if dims.z>.45:
00287:             obj.data.materials.clear()

00022: def add(parent, name, node):
00023:     parent.include(name, node)
00024:     return node
00025: 
00026: def ring(parent, name, center, radius, tube, plane="xz",
00027:          color=chrome, steps=24):
00028:     pts = []
00029:     for i in range(steps):
00030:         a = 2 * math.pi * i / steps
00031:         if plane == "yz":
00032:             p = (center[0],
00033:                  center[1] + radius * math.cos(a),
00034:                  center[2] + radius * math.sin(a))
00035:         elif plane == "xy":
00036:             p = (center[0] + radius * math.cos(a),
00037:                  center[1] + radius * math.sin(a),
00038:                  center[2])
00039:         else:
00040:             p = (center[0] + radius * math.cos(a),
00041:                  center[1],
00042:                  center[2] + radius * math.sin(a))
00043:         pts.append(p)
00044:     for i in range(steps):
00045:         add(parent, name + str(i),
00046:             rod(radius=tube, start=pts[i],
00047:                 end=pts[(i + 1) % steps], color=color))

00293: def noise_material(name, colors, scale, roughness=.7, bump_strength=0):
00294:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00295:     mat.use_nodes = True
00296:     nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00297:     links = mat.node_tree.links
00298:     bs = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00299:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00300:     tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00301:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = scale
00302:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2.0
00303:     ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00304:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .25
00305:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*colors[0], 1)
00306:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .77
00307:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*colors[1], 1)
00308:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], ramp.inputs["Fac"])
00309:     links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00310:     if bump_strength:
00311:         bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00312:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = bump_strength
00313:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .002
00314:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00315:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00316:     return mat
00317: 
00318: granite = noise_material(

00320:     ((.075, .070, .055), (.56, .51, .40)), 210, .64, .22)

00322:     "Subtle paper texture",
00323:     ((.83, .80, .70), (.96, .93, .84)), 145, .92, .08)

00325: def world_bounds(obj):
00326:     corners = [obj.matrix_world @ Vector(p) for p in obj.bound_box]
00327:     low = Vector(tuple(min(p[i] for p in corners) for i in range(3)))
00328:     high = Vector(tuple(max(p[i] for p in corners) for i in range(3)))
00329:     return (low + high) * .5, high - low
00330: 
00331: # World geometry bounds handle meshes whose origins are at the scene origin.
00332: # Identify the two architectural slabs explicitly by their dimensions and elevation.
00333: for obj in list(bpy.context.scene.objects):
00334:     if obj.type != "MESH":
00335:         continue

00339:         and abs(dims.y - COUNTER_SIZE[1]) < .015
00340:         and abs(center.z - .89) < .015)

00342:         abs(dims.x - 2.65) < .015
00343:         and abs(dims.y - .025) < .01
00344:         and abs(center.z - .995) < .015)

00349:           and abs(center.z - rollz) < .08
00350:           and dims.z > .45):

00354:     # Soften only the connected cloth silhouette.

00364:     for mat in obj.data.materials:

00019:     parent.include(name, node)

00022: def ring(parent, name, center, radius, tube, plane="xz",
00023:          color=chrome, steps=24):
00024:     pts = []
00025:     for i in range(steps):
00026:         a = 2 * math.pi * i / steps
00027:         c, s = radius * math.cos(a), radius * math.sin(a)
00028:         if plane == "yz":
00029:             p = (center[0], center[1]+c, center[2]+s)
00030:         elif plane == "xy":
00031:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1]+s, center[2])
00032:         else:
00033:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1], center[2]+s)
00034:         pts.append(p)
00035:     result = Asset(name)
00036:     for i in range(steps):
00037:         add(result, "segment_%d" % i,
00038:             rod(radius=tube, start=pts[i],
00039:                 end=pts[(i+1) % steps], color=color))
00040:     add(parent, name, result)
00041:     return result

00278: def noise_material(name, colors, scale, roughness=.7, bump_strength=0):
00279:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00280:     mat.use_nodes = True
00281:     n, links = mat.node_tree.nodes, mat.node_tree.links
00282:     bs = n.get("Principled BSDF")
00283:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00284:     tex = n.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00285:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = scale
00286:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00287:     ramp = n.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00288:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .35
00289:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*colors[0], 1)
00290:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .65
00291:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*colors[1], 1)
00292:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], ramp.inputs["Fac"])
00293:     links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00294:     if bump_strength:
00295:         bump = n.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00296:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = bump_strength
00297:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .001
00298:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00299:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00300:     return mat
00301:

00312: def world_bounds(obj):
00313:     points = [obj.matrix_world @ Vector(p) for p in obj.bound_box]
00314:     low = Vector(tuple(min(p[i] for p in points) for i in range(3)))
00315:     high = Vector(tuple(max(p[i] for p in points) for i in range(3)))
00316:     return (low+high)*.5, high-low
00317: 
00318: for obj in list(bpy.context.scene.objects):
00319:     if obj.type != "MESH":
00320:         continue
00321:     center, dims = world_bounds(obj)
00322:     counter = (abs(dims.x-2.65)<.015 and
00323:                abs(dims.y-1.30)<.015 and abs(center.z-.89)<.015)
00324:     splash = (abs(dims.x-2.65)<.015 and
00325:               abs(dims.y-.025)<.01 and abs(center.z-.995)<.015)
00326:     if counter or splash:
00327:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00328:         obj.data.materials.append(granite)
00329:     elif (abs(center.x-rollx)<.12 and abs(center.z-rollz)<.08
00330:           and dims.z>.45):
00331:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00332:         obj.data.materials.append(paper_mat)
00333: 
00334:     # Replace the cloth proxy within its existing assembly by a folded surface.
00335:     if (abs(center.x-.10)<.005 and abs(center.y+.145)<.005
00336:         and abs(dims.x-.24)<.005 and abs(dims.z-.55)<.005):
00337:         verts, faces = [], []
00338:         cols, rows = 24, 18
00339:         for j in range(rows+1):
00340:             v = j/rows
00341:             for i in range(cols+1):
00342:                 u = i/cols
00343:                 width = .145 + .095*math.sin(math.pi*v*.78)
00344:                 x = .10 + (u-.5)*width - .035*v
00345:                 hem = .045*math.cos(u*math.pi*2) + .02*u
00346:                 z = 1.72 - v*(.57+hem)
00347:                 y = -.145 + .022*math.sin(u*math.pi*7+.5*v)
00348:                 y -= .018*math.sin(math.pi*v)
00349:                 verts.append((x, y, z))
00350:         for j in range(rows):
00351:             for i in range(cols):

00211:         mat.use_nodes = True
